| 002 | German corporate income tax (Körperschaftsteuer): 2026 LF[20, 20] | Tax year: the calendar year 2026 (Veranlagungszeitraum 2026). A company with a financial year that does not match the calendar year is taxed on the profit of the financial year that ends in 2026 ([§ 7(4) KStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/kstg_1977/__7.html)). A German company pays three taxes on its profit: corporate income tax, the solidarity surcharge on that tax, and municipal trade tax. This skill covers the first two in full and summarises the third. For the trade tax method, use `de-trade-tax`. | Preserved routing/presentation/express limitation/attribution; no new positive rule.. Exact predecessor current unit, native coordinates rebound |
| 003 | Who this applies to LF[24, 24] | - **Resident companies (unlimited tax liability).** A company is taxed in Germany on its worldwide income if its place of management (Geschäftsleitung) or its registered seat (Sitz) is in Germany ([§ 1(1) and (2) KStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/kstg_1977/__1.html)). This includes the GmbH (and its UG variant), the AG, the SE, the KGaA, cooperatives, other private-law legal persons, and commercial businesses of public bodies. "Place of management" means the centre of top-level management ([§ 10 AO](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ao_1977/__10.html)); the seat is the place named in the articles ([§ 11 AO](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ao_1977/__11.html)). A foreign-registered company run from Germany is therefore fully taxable here. | [de-corporate-tax.KStG1-1](#q-6cda8b1fed770d), [de-corporate-tax.KStG1-2](#q-a5ed937b3449fd), [de-corporate-tax.AO10](#q-d210d0851e90d7), [de-corporate-tax.AO11](#q-c3b6121b622d00). Exact predecessor current unit, native coordinates rebound |
| 004 | Who this applies to LF[25, 25] | - **Non-resident companies (limited tax liability).** A company with neither its management nor its seat in Germany is taxed only on German-source income ([§ 2 no. 1 KStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/kstg_1977/__2.html)). The main case is a German permanent establishment (Betriebsstätte): any fixed place of business, such as a branch, office, workshop, warehouse, or a building site lasting more than six months ([§ 12 AO](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ao_1977/__12.html)). A treaty may narrow this. | [de-corporate-tax.KStG2-1](#q-1199a7a845d9de), [de-corporate-tax.AO12](#q-f37979b2cad719). Exact predecessor current unit, native coordinates rebound |

| 029 | Rates, bands and thresholds LF[55, 55] | **What applies for 2026, plainly.** The rate is 15% for 2026 and 2027. The law of 14 July 2025 for an immediate tax investment programme (Gesetz für ein steuerliches Investitionssofortprogramm) wrote the cuts into § 23 KStG; the first cut is for 2028. Do not apply 14% or lower to any 2026 or 2027 income. | [de-corporate-tax.KStG23-1](#q-45365b31919e6e), [de-corporate-tax.BG161date](#q-713fd282cb950a), [de-corporate-tax.BG161rate](#q-e7963a61d7a521). Exact predecessor current unit, native coordinates rebound |
| 030 | Rates, bands and thresholds LF[57, 57] | **Trade tax in one paragraph.** Every GmbH or AG is a trade business in full ([§ 2(2) GewStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/gewstg/__2.html)). Trade income starts from the corporate tax profit, then adds back part of financing costs (interest, rent, leases, royalties above a EUR 200,000 allowance) and makes reductions (for example dividends from holdings of at least 15%). It is rounded down to full EUR 100, multiplied by 3.5%, then by the multiplier of each municipality where the company has an establishment. At a 400% multiplier the trade tax is 14% of trade income, which with 15.825% gives 29.825% in total. Multipliers differ widely by town: always read the client's own notice. A company gets no credit for trade tax against corporate tax (the § 35 EStG credit is for individuals only). Method, add-backs and reductions: see `de-trade-tax`. | [de-corporate-tax.G2](#q-66d881dbc91dee), [de-corporate-tax.G7](#q-8edd8a2ebade39), [de-corporate-tax.G8](#q-21f1da77325174), [de-corporate-tax.G9](#q-96ce9d257a6e64), [de-corporate-tax.G11](#q-e3cee057d5ff04), [de-corporate-tax.G16](#q-834e5c030e525e), [de-corporate-tax.KStG23-1](#q-45365b31919e6e), [de-corporate-tax.SoliRate](#q-0658294950438e), [de-corporate-tax.E35](#q-c5c4830683d835). Exact predecessor current unit, native coordinates rebound |

For a GmbH or AG all income is business income ([§ 8(2) KStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/kstg_1977/__8.html)). | [de-corporate-tax.KStG8-1](#q-6a3495e1cedc74), [de-corporate-tax.KStG8-2](#q-c270927599ef3d). | 039 | The taxable base LF[70, 70] |    - Dividends received are exempt, but 5% is added back as a non-deductible expense (§ 8b(1) and (5)). The direct holding test is at least 10% at the start of the calendar year. Under § 8b(4) sentence 6, acquisition of a participation of at least 10% is deemed to occur at the start of that year. Where the holding test, including that deeming rule, is not met, dividends are fully taxable (§ 8b(4)). | [de-corporate-tax.KStG8b-1](#q-5e2d3d95f71040), [de-corporate-tax.KStG8b-4](#q-d944199029118b), [de-corporate-tax.KStG8b-5](#q-83cd98331a6ae7). | 043 | The taxable base LF[74, 75] |    - Interest expense is deductible up to interest income. Net interest above that is deductible only up to 30% of tax EBITDA. After using eligible EBITDA carries from the previous five financial years in chronological order, remaining nondeductible interest is carried forward (Zinsvortrag). Unused current EBITDA capacity is carried into the following five financial years, but no EBITDA carry is generated in a year when interest expense does not exceed interest income or an Absatz 2 escape excludes the barrier (§ 4h(1) sentences 3–6). The statutory chronological rule concerns EBITDA carry vintages; this summary does not supply a complete administrative allocation worksheet for a year mixing old interest carry and current interest.<br>   - The rule does not apply when net interest is **less than** EUR 3,000,000 (§ 4h(2)(a)). This is a cliff, not an allowance: at EUR 3,000,000 or more, the whole net interest is tested. Absatz 2 does not apply insofar as interest expense is increased by an old interest carry (§ 4h(1) sentence 7); do not exempt that old carry under the current-year escape. | [de-corporate-tax.E4h-1](#q-26ce8f98dfe470), [R07.interest](#q-cbc3e66545306d), [R07.corporate-income](#q-8fb31dbc1e6369). Exact predecessor current unit, native coordinates rebound |
| 044 | The taxable base LF[74, 75] |    - Interest expense is deductible up to interest income. Net interest above that is deductible only up to 30% of tax EBITDA. After using eligible EBITDA carries from the previous five financial years in chronological order, remaining nondeductible interest is carried forward (Zinsvortrag). Unused current EBITDA capacity is carried into the following five financial years, but no EBITDA carry is generated in a year when interest expense does not exceed interest income or an Absatz 2 escape excludes the barrier (§ 4h(1) sentences 3–6). The statutory chronological rule concerns EBITDA carry vintages; this summary does not supply a complete administrative allocation worksheet for a year mixing old interest carry and current interest.<br>   - The rule does not apply when net interest is **less than** EUR 3,000,000 (§ 4h(2)(a)). This is a cliff, not an allowance: at EUR 3,000,000 or more, the whole net interest is tested. Absatz 2 does not apply insofar as interest expense is increased by an old interest carry (§ 4h(1) sentence 7); do not exempt that old carry under the current-year escape. | [de-corporate-tax.E4h-2](#q-29762b086cf0ec), [R07.interest](#q-cbc3e66545306d), [R07.corporate-income](#q-8fb31dbc1e6369). | 047 | The taxable base LF[78, 78] |    - Carry-back: up to EUR 1,000,000 to the previous year, then to the year before that (§ 10d(1)). The company can ask to skip carry-back in full. | [de-corporate-tax.E10d-1](#q-f7117c16207770). Exact predecessor current unit, native coordinates rebound |
| 048 | The taxable base LF[79, 79] |    - Carry-forward: unlimited in time. Each year, the first EUR 1,000,000 of income can be fully offset, then 70% of the income above EUR 1,000,000 (§ 10d(2), for 2024 to 2027 under § 52(18b)). So at least 30% of income above EUR 1m is always taxed (minimum taxation). From 2028 the share returns to 60%: Art. 6 of the law of 27 March 2024 ([BGBl. 2024 I Nr. 108](https://www.recht.bund.de/bgbl/1/2024/108/VO.html)) is already enacted and comes into force on 1 January 2028. The consolidated statute page does not show it yet. Plan multi-year loss use with 60% from 2028. | [de-corporate-tax.E10d-2](#q-8d0ea438a3a31e), [de-corporate-tax.E52loss](#q-2d8c9d1d90d0e3), [de-corporate-tax.BG108a](#q-1e14f5292d4e2a), [de-corporate-tax.BG108date](#q-b4a706aa1874ef). | 050 | The taxable base LF[81, 81] | 7. **Share changes ([§ 8c KStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/kstg_1977/__8c.html)).** If more than 50% of the shares or voting rights pass to one buyer (or a group with common interests) within five years, all unused losses so far are lost. A company that keeps the same business can apply to keep losses under [§ 8d KStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/kstg_1977/__8d.html); the application goes in the return for the year of the transfer. | [de-corporate-tax.KStG8c-1](#q-8f71a909253553), [de-corporate-tax.KStG8d-1](#q-bf7ba632415d60). | 118 | Example 2: losses brought forward plus a dividend from a subsidiary LF[173, 173] | 4. **Trade tax.** Trade income starts from the same EUR 2,810,000. The dividend is already out through § 8b, and the 5% add-back stays in trade income ([§ 9 no. 2a sentence 4 GewStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/gewstg/__9.html)). Loss offset: EUR 1,000,000 plus **60%** of EUR 1,810,000 = EUR 2,086,000. Trade income EUR 724,000; base amount at 3.5% = EUR 25,340; trade tax at 400% = EUR 101,360. Trade tax losses left: EUR 1,914,000. | [de-corporate-tax.KStG10](#q-3461ed9e464195), [de-corporate-tax.KStG23-1](#q-45365b31919e6e), [de-corporate-tax.SoliRate](#q-0658294950438e), [de-corporate-tax.G11](#q-e3cee057d5ff04), [de-corporate-tax.G16](#q-834e5c030e525e), [de-corporate-tax.KStG8b-1](#q-5e2d3d95f71040), [de-corporate-tax.KStG8b-4](#q-d944199029118b), [de-corporate-tax.KStG8b-5](#q-83cd98331a6ae7), [de-corporate-tax.E10d-2](#q-8d0ea438a3a31e), [de-corporate-tax.E52loss](#q-2d8c9d1d90d0e3), [de-corporate-tax.G10a](#q-0db9eb3bfdf1e0), [de-corporate-tax.G9](#q-96ce9d257a6e64), [de-corporate-tax.E36-4](#q-46992b5e7861eb). | 232 | The taxable base / final assessed KSt LF[84, 84] | 9. **Corporate-tax assessment rounding.** Round each individual assessed corporate-tax amount in the taxpayer's favour to whole euros ([§ 31(1) sentence 2 KStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/kstg_1977/__31.html)). For an ordinary positive amount this means rounding down: stipulated taxable income of EUR 10,001 at 15% gives EUR 1,500.15 before rounding and assessed KSt of EUR 1,500. This is the assessed KSt step; underlying bases and surcharges retain their own rules. | [R09.assessment-rounding](#q-093d3d70cf87d1), [de-corporate-tax.KStG23](#q-45365b31919e6e). Exact predecessor current unit, native coordinates rebound |

Every predecessor historical unit remains in support/de-corporate-tax/old-topic-destinations.json, including all earlier wording/hashes and destination history.

| Historical reference / LF | Exact topic / opening | Current destination and bounded correction |
|---|---|---|
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF18 | > **General reference only.** This skill is a source-cited draft. No accountant has reviewed it. Do not rely on it to file, pay or take a tax position without review by a qualified German tax adviser (Steuerberater). | Exact wording retained; current LF18, German corporate income tax (Körperschaftsteuer): 2026   |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF20 | Tax year: the calendar year 2026 (Veranlagungszeitraum 2026). A company with a financial year that does not match the calendar year is taxed on the profit of the financial year that ends in 2026 ([§ 7(4) KStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/kstg_1977/__7.html)). A German company pays three taxes on its profit: corporate income tax, the solidarity surcharge on that tax, and municipal trade tax. This skill covers the first two in full and summarises the third. For the trade tax method, use `de-trade-tax`. | Exact wording retained; current LF20, German corporate income tax (Körperschaftsteuer): 2026   |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF24 | - **Resident companies (unlimited tax liability).** A company is taxed in Germany on its worldwide income if its place of management (Geschäftsleitung) or its registered seat (Sitz) is in Germany ([§ 1(1) and (2) KStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/kstg_1977/__1.html)). This includes the GmbH (and its UG variant), the AG, the SE, the KGaA, cooperatives, other private-law legal persons, and commercial businesses of public bodies. "Place of management" means the centre of top-level management ([§ 10 AO](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ao_1977/__10.html)); the seat is the place named in the articles ([§ 11 AO](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ao_1977/__11.html)). A foreign-registered company run from Germany is therefore fully taxable here. | Exact wording retained; current LF24, Who this applies to   |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF25 | - **Non-resident companies (limited tax liability).** A company with neither its management nor its seat in Germany is taxed only on German-source income ([§ 2 no. 1 KStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/kstg_1977/__2.html)). The main case is a German permanent establishment (Betriebsstätte): any fixed place of business, such as a branch, office, workshop, warehouse, or a building site lasting more than six months ([§ 12 AO](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ao_1977/__12.html)). A treaty may narrow this. | Exact wording retained; current LF25, Who this applies to   |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF26 | - **Out of scope here:** | Exact wording retained; current LF26, Who this applies to   |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF27 |   - Partnerships (OHG, KG, GmbH & Co. KG) are not subject to corporate tax unless they opt in under § 1a KStG. Their partners are taxed. A partnership does pay trade tax. | Exact wording retained; current LF27, Who this applies to   |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF28 |   - Bodies exempt under [§ 5 KStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/kstg_1977/__5.html) (for example charities) are not covered. | Exact wording retained; current LF28, Who this applies to   |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF29 |   - Banks, insurers, investment funds and REITs have special rules. Refer. | Exact wording retained; current LF29, Who this applies to   |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF30 |   - Groups with consolidated revenue of EUR 750 million or more also fall under the minimum tax law (see the rates table). This skill does not compute top-up tax. | Exact wording retained; current LF30, Who this applies to   |

| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF55 | **What applies for 2026, plainly.** The rate is 15% for 2026 and 2027. The law of 14 July 2025 for an immediate tax investment programme (Gesetz für ein steuerliches Investitionssofortprogramm) wrote the cuts into § 23 KStG; the first cut is for 2028. Do not apply 14% or lower to any 2026 or 2027 income. | Exact wording retained; current LF55, Rates, bands and thresholds   |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF57 | **Trade tax in one paragraph.** Every GmbH or AG is a trade business in full ([§ 2(2) GewStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/gewstg/__2.html)). Trade income starts from the corporate tax profit, then adds back part of financing costs (interest, rent, leases, royalties above a EUR 200,000 allowance) and makes reductions (for example dividends from holdings of at least 15%). It is rounded down to full EUR 100, multiplied by 3.5%, then by the multiplier of each municipality where the company has an establishment. At a 400% multiplier the trade tax is 14% of trade income, which with 15.825% gives 29.825% in total. Multipliers differ widely by town: always read the client's own notice. A company gets no credit for trade tax against corporate tax (the § 35 EStG credit is for individuals only). Method, add-backs and reductions: see `de-trade-tax`. | Exact wording retained; current LF57, Rates, bands and thresholds   |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF61 | Income is worked out under the Income Tax Act and the KStG together ([§ 8(1) KStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/kstg_1977/__8.html)). For a GmbH or AG all income is business income ([§ 8(2) KStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/kstg_1977/__8.html)). | Exact wording retained; current LF61, The taxable base   |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF63 | 1. **Start** from the profit in the tax balance sheet (commercial accounts adjusted to tax rules). | Exact wording retained; current LF63, The taxable base   |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF64 | 2. **Add back non-deductible items:** | Exact wording retained; current LF64, The taxable base   |

| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF66 |    - Half of supervisory board fees ([§ 10 no. 4 KStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/kstg_1977/__10.html)). | Exact wording retained; current LF66, The taxable base   |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF67 |    - Criminal fines and similar ([§ 10 no. 3 KStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/kstg_1977/__10.html)) and the other limits in § 4(5) EStG (for example part of gifts and entertainment). | Exact wording retained; current LF67, The taxable base   |

They do not reduce income ([§ 8(3) KStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/kstg_1977/__8.html)). Only if the holding was at least 10% directly at the start of the calendar year; below that, dividends are fully taxable (§ 8b(4)). | DE-L02 source correction: retained dividend holding topic, now adds §8b(4) sentence6 qualifying-acquisition deeming; current LF70. | 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF71 |    - Gains on selling shares in a company are exempt with no minimum holding, again with 5% added back (§ 8b(2) and (3)). Losses and write-downs on such shares are not deductible (§ 8b(3)), and the same can apply to loans from a shareholder holding more than 25%. | Exact wording retained; current LF71, The taxable base   |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF72 | 4. **Depreciation.** Straight-line over useful life ([§ 7(1) EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__7.html)). For movable assets acquired from 1 July 2025 to 31 December 2027 the company may choose declining balance at up to three times the straight-line rate, max 30% (§ 7(2) EStG). Items up to EUR 800 net can be written off at once ([§ 6(2) EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__6.html)). | Exact wording retained; current LF72, The taxable base   |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF73 | 5. **Interest barrier ([§ 4h EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__4h.html), [§ 8a KStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/kstg_1977/__8a.html)).** | Exact wording retained; current LF73, The taxable base   |

Interest that cannot be deducted is carried forward (Zinsvortrag). Unused EBITDA capacity is carried forward five years. | Exact wording retained; current LF74, The taxable base   |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF75 |    - The rule does not apply when net interest is **less than** EUR 3,000,000 (§ 4h(2)(a)). This is a cliff, not an allowance: at EUR 3,000,000 or more, the whole net interest is tested. | Exact wording retained; current LF74, The taxable base   |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF76 |    - Other escapes: the company is not related to anyone and has no foreign PE (§ 4h(2)(b)), or the equity ratio test for group members (§ 4h(2)(c), restricted for companies by § 8a(3) KStG where shareholder debt is more than 10% of net interest). | Exact wording retained; current LF76, The taxable base   |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF77 | 6. **Losses ([§ 10d EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__10d.html), applied to companies through § 8(1) KStG).** | Exact wording retained; current LF77, The taxable base   |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF78 |    - Carry-back: up to EUR 1,000,000 to the previous year, then to the year before that (§ 10d(1)). The company can ask to skip carry-back in full. | Exact wording retained; current LF78, The taxable base   |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF79 |    - Carry-forward: unlimited in time. Each year, the first EUR 1,000,000 of income can be fully offset, then 70% of the income above EUR 1,000,000 (§ 10d(2), for 2024 to 2027 under § 52(18b)). So at least 30% of income above EUR 1m is always taxed (minimum taxation). From 2028 the share returns to 60%: Art. 6 of the law of 27 March 2024 ([BGBl. 2024 I Nr. 108](https://www.recht.bund.de/bgbl/1/2024/108/VO.html)) is already enacted and comes into force on 1 January 2028. The consolidated statute page does not show it yet. Plan multi-year loss use with 60% from 2028. | Exact wording retained; current LF79, The taxable base   |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF80 |    - Trade tax has its own loss account: EUR 1m plus **60%**, and no carry-back ([§ 10a GewStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/gewstg/__10a.html)). Do not assume the two loss figures match. | Exact wording retained; current LF80, The taxable base   |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF81 | 7. **Share changes ([§ 8c KStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/kstg_1977/__8c.html)).** If more than 50% of the shares or voting rights pass to one buyer (or a group with common interests) within five years, all unused losses so far are lost. Exceptions: transfers inside a 100% group (the group clause), and losses up to the built-in gains (stille Reserven) taxable in Germany. A company that keeps the same business can apply to keep losses under [§ 8d KStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/kstg_1977/__8d.html); the application goes in the return for the year of the transfer. | Exact wording retained; current LF81, The taxable base   |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF82 | 8. **Tax group (Organschaft) ([§ 14 KStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/kstg_1977/__14.html), [§ 17 KStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/kstg_1977/__17.html)).** The subsidiary's income is attributed to the parent if: the parent holds the majority of votes without break from the start of the subsidiary's financial year; the subsidiary signs a profit transfer agreement (Gewinnabführungsvertrag) for at least five years and actually carries it out; and the holding belongs to a German PE of the parent. A GmbH subsidiary must also agree to loss assumption by reference to § 302 AktG (§ 17 KStG). For trade tax the subsidiary is treated as a PE of the parent ([§ 2(2) GewStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/gewstg/__2.html)). There is no other form of group relief. | Exact wording retained; current LF82, The taxable base   |

| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF88 | \| Return \| Corporate tax return (Körperschaftsteuererklärung), plus the trade tax return and, where relevant, the separate determinations (for example the tax contribution account, § 27 KStG). Filed electronically in the official data format via the official interface (ELSTER). Paper only on application for hardship. In ELSTER the return is form KSt 1 (a cover form) with annexes, for example Anlage GK (business income), Anlage ZVE (taxable income), Anlage Verluste (losses), Anlage Zinsschranke (interest barrier), Anlagen OG and OT (tax group) and Anlage KSt 1 F (tax contribution account). The anti-treaty-shopping rule in [§ 50d(3) EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__50d.html) applies. | Exact wording retained; current LF113, Cross-border points   |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF112 |   - **Exemption certificate:** the payer must withhold unless the shareholder holds a Freistellungsbescheinigung from the Federal Central Tax Office (BZSt) under [§ 50c(2) no. 1 EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__50c.html). It runs at the earliest from the day the application reaches the BZSt, for at most five years, and cannot be backdated ([BZSt: exemption procedure](https://www.bzst.de/DE/Unternehmen/Kapitalertraege/Kapitalertragsteuerentlastung/Sonderkonstellation/Freistellungsverfahren_50c/freistellungsverfahren_50c_node.html)). Otherwise the shareholder claims a refund from the BZSt under § 50c(3) EStG. | Exact wording retained; current LF114, Cross-border points   |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF113 |   - **Any foreign company with no treaty or directive relief:** two fifths of the 25% withheld is refunded ([§ 44a(9) EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__44a.html)), leaving 15% plus surcharge. Treaty rates may be lower, claimed through the same BZSt procedures. | Exact wording retained; current LF115, Cross-border points   |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF114 | - **Interest.** Withholding under § 43 EStG covers specific types, for example convertible bonds, profit-participating bonds and profit participation rights ([§ 43(1) no. 2 EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__43.html)), at 25%. Ordinary loan interest paid to a foreign lender is not in that list, but check the lender's limited tax liability under § 49 EStG before paying gross. Interest between associated EU companies can be relieved under [§ 50g EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__50g.html) (Interest and Royalties Directive). | Exact wording retained; current LF116, Cross-border points   |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF115 | - **Royalties.** Payments to a non-resident for the use of rights or know-how bear 15% withholding ([§ 50a(1) no. 3, (2) EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__50a.html)) plus 5.5% surcharge ([§ 3(1) no. 6 SolzG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/solzg_1995/__3.html)). Relief through § 50g EStG, a treaty, or a BZSt exemption certificate. Where a treaty applies and the payments to that recipient in the calendar year are not more than EUR 10,000, the payer need not withhold but must still file the withholding return (§ 50c(2) no. 2 EStG). | Exact wording retained; current LF117, Cross-border points   |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF116 | - **For the non-resident recipient,** corporate tax on income subject to withholding is generally settled by the withholding, unless the income belongs to a German business ([§ 32(1) KStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/kstg_1977/__32.html)). | Exact wording retained; current LF118, Cross-border points   |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF117 | - **CFC rules (Hinzurechnungsbesteuerung).** If German taxpayers control a foreign company, its passive income is added to their German income where it bears tax of less than 15% ([§ 7 AStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/astg/__7.html), [§ 8(5) AStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/astg/__8.html)). The test compares the foreign tax with 15%, not with the German rate, so the 2028 to 2032 rate cuts do not change it. Refer any CFC case. | Exact wording retained; current LF119, Cross-border points   |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF118 | - **Exit tax for companies.** If Germany loses or restricts its right to tax the gain on an asset (for example the asset moves to a foreign PE), the company is treated as selling it at fair market value ([§ 12(1) KStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/kstg_1977/__12.html)). For a move to an EU or EEA state the company can apply for a compensating item released over five years ([§ 4g EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__4g.html)). Shareholders who leave Germany: see `leaving-germany-exit-tax`. | Exact wording retained; current LF120, Cross-border points   |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF124 | A GmbH sells machinery parts from one site. Its financial year is the calendar year 2026. Profit before tax in the accounts is EUR 480,000. It paid EUR 36,000 in supervisory board fees and a criminal-court fine of EUR 2,000 (both expensed). No dividends, no losses, net interest well under EUR 3m. Assumed trade tax multiplier: 400% (illustration only; read the real one from the municipality). No trade tax add-backs or reductions apply. | Exact wording retained; current LF126, Example 1: trading GmbH, profit EUR 480,000 before tax   |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF126 | 1. Add back half the board fees, EUR 18,000, and the fine, EUR 2,000. Taxable income: 480,000 + 18,000 + 2,000 = EUR 500,000. | Exact wording retained; current LF128, Example 1: trading GmbH, profit EUR 480,000 before tax   |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF127 | 2. Corporate tax at 15%: EUR 75,000. Solidarity surcharge at 5.5%: EUR 4,125. | Exact wording retained; current LF129, Example 1: trading GmbH, profit EUR 480,000 before tax   |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF128 | 3. Trade income EUR 500,000 (already a multiple of EUR 100, no allowance for a company). Base amount at 3.5%: EUR 17,500. | 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF166 | A holding and trading GmbH has, for 2026, profit before tax of EUR 3,000,000. | 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF168 | 1. **Participation exemption.** The holding was at least 10% at the start of 2026, so the dividend is exempt: deduct EUR 200,000, add back 5% = EUR 10,000. | 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF170 | 3. **Corporate tax and surcharge.** 15% of EUR 543,000 = EUR 81,450. Surcharge 5.5% = EUR 4,479.75. | Exact wording retained; current LF172, Example 2: losses brought forward plus a dividend from a subsidiary   |

| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF211 | 1. **Where is the company managed, and where are its establishments?** This decides German residence, and the trade tax multiplier of each municipality (with its year). Several towns means the base amount is split. | Exact wording retained; current LF213, Ask the client first   |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF212 | 2. **What is the financial year?** A non-calendar year changes which profit falls into 2026 and when prepayments are due. | Exact wording retained; current LF214, Ask the client first   |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF213 | 3. **Is a Steuerberater filing the return?** It moves the deadline from 2 August 2027 to 29 February 2028, unless the tax office orders earlier filing. | Exact wording retained; current LF215, Ask the client first   |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF214 | 4. **What shareholdings does the company hold, how large, and since when?** At least 10% at 1 January for the corporate tax dividend exemption; at least 15% at the start of the period for the trade tax reduction; 12 months for § 43b relief on dividends it pays out. | DE-L02 source correction: retained dividend holding topic, now adds §8b(4) sentence6 qualifying-acquisition deeming; current LF216. Historical absolute start-of-year-only formulation corrected, not dropped.   |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF215 | 5. **Have more than 50% of its own shares changed hands in the last five years, or is that planned?** Losses may be forfeited under § 8c KStG. | Exact wording retained; current LF217, Ask the client first   |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF216 | 6. **What is the net interest expense, and is the company part of a group or tax group?** At EUR 3m or more the interest barrier applies; an Organschaft changes who is taxed. | Exact wording retained; current LF218, Ask the client first   |
